Let me think carefully about this problem. We need minimize evaluate_sequence over non-negative (real) sequences:

score = 2 n max_conv / (sum_a)^2，where max_conv = max of full convolution sequence * sequence (linear convolution, length 2n-1).

Note: evaluator internally clips each element to [0, 1000]. If input sum exceeds 0.01 it's valid. Score is scale-invariant (multiplying the whole sequence by a constant cancels out max and sum squared, so 2 n max_b / sum^2 is invariant to scaling). So only the shape matters. Also safe with the 1000 clipping: the maximum element after scaling should ideally be below 1000. If the sum is huge and the peak is tiny... we can just normalize so max element is at most, say, 100.

So the real problem is: minimize ratio 2n * ||a * a||_∞ / (Σ a)^2 over a ≥ 0, a ∈ R^n (effectively length is up to our choice but we should keep compute tractable, and the provided construction length is 6962; we can choose another length).

Target upper bound is 1.5027, and score of the best known state is 2.0 (the one-of sort with length 6962). On the target length it is said that "construct length: 6962". That is, the previous program used length 6962 and got 2.000000 (unimodal leading?).

Let's think about theoretical lower bounds. Scaled Cauchy–Schwarz: max_b ≥ (Σ a)^2 / (2n-1) → score ≥ 2n/(2n-1) → ~1. So absolute lower bound is close to 1. If the convolution is completely uniform then score → 1: a * a is uniform only if... full convolution has 2n-1 elements and the sum of convolution = (Σ a)^2, so average is (Σa)^2/(2n-1) ≥ … score ≥ 2n/(2n-1). So uniform convolution (Barker-like with non-negativity) is the ideal: score close to 1. The ideal object is a non-negative autocorrelation side-peaks: a * a (embedding a = [a, 0] into the cyclic of size 2n-1? No, full convolution is not circular) being flat.

This is like a "cost function with zero autocorrelation" but non-negative: it's like asking whether a non-negative quasi-orthogonal sequence basically exists. The ideal 1 is unimodal.

Known results: this is the "minimum peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR)" problem on linear convolution. Since non-negative, the classical flat-top window... let's think: if a is all constant 1's, then a*a = triangular ramp with max n, sum n → score = 2n*n/n^2 = 2. It's exactly the 2.0 that the previous program achieved (maybe it barely improved from the 1's and converged to 2.0).

Since low-frequency efficiency of the non-negative constraint, we can do better. For example, aperiodic "Golay-like" non-negative (integer) sequences with low autocorrelation? The score involves max of the full linear autocorrelation.

```python
"""
Active-set subgradient descent with a bank of analytic prior designs.

Objective: minimize 2n*max(b)/S^2, b = full self-convolution of the
nonnegative heights.  b is positive definite and symmetric, so the active
set is (n-1-L, n-1, n-1+L) for L = 0 .. floor(n/2).  We monitor all 2n-1
diagonals cheaply via FFT, take the largest fewactive indices, apply the
exact per-coordinate subgradient of b_k, keep a zero-mean momentum, and do
a small line search.  Prior "designs" (linear ramp, cos bell, half-sine,
trapezoids, smoothed randoms, parent blends) seed many short descents; the
survivors are refined longer, and finally the current champion is polished
to the deadline.  The current best is logged every second (no lambda, all
helper functions top-level) and the global best valid sequence is always
the return value; a safe fallback is computed analytically via FFT as well.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np


_global_state = {"best": None}


def clamp_sequence(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0 or float(arr.sum()) < 0.02:
        return None
    return arr


def next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def convolution_full(a):
    n = a.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    h = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(h * h, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    return b


def score_info(a):
    n = a.size
    b = convolution_full(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return math.inf, float("inf"), np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
    order = np.argsort(b)
    idx = np.array(order[::-1][: min(24, b.size)], dtype=int)
    t = float(b[idx[0]])
    score = float(2.0 * n * t / (s * s))
    return score, t, idx


def active_indices(best_idx, K, n):
    K = max(3, int(K))
    out = []
    n1 = n - 1
    for k in best_idx[: K]:
        L = abs(int(k) - n1)
        if L not in out:
            out.append(L)
        if len(out) * 2 >= n:
            break
    if len(out) == 0:
        out = list(range(min(3, n)))
    outs = []
    for L in out[:K]:
        outs.append(n1 - L)
        if L > 0:
            outs.append(n1 + L)
        if L < 0:
            outs.append(n1 - L)
    return outs


def subgrad_step(a, best_idx, K, n, nstep):
    """Sum of b_k subgradients over the largest K distinct lags |k-(n-1)|."""
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    n1 = n - 1
    nstep = max(1, int(nstep))
    for L in active_indices(best_idx, K, n):
        for kk in (n1 - L, n1 + L):
            lo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, kk)
            if hi < lo:
                continue
            vals = a[hi::-1][: max(1, hi - lo + 1)]
            vals = vals[: (hi - lo + 1)]
            seg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            seg[lo : lo + vals.size] = vals[: (hi - lo + 1)]
            g += np.roll(seg, nstep)
    g -= g.mean()
    return g


def smooth_kernel(w):
    w = max(1, int(w))
    r = w // 2
    x = np.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    k = 1.0 / np.sqrt(2.0 * math.pi) * 0.9
    kern = np.exp(-(x * x) / (2.0 * (max(0.7, 0.9 * r) ** 2)))
    kern /= kern.sum()
    return kern


def smooth1d(v, w):
    kern = smooth_kernel(w)
    return np.convolve(v, kern, mode="same")


def normalize_sum1(a):
    t = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(t) or t <= 1e-12:
        return a
    return a / t


def save_best(a, s):
    if _global_state["best"] is None or s < _global_state.get("best_score", math.inf):
        _global_state["best"] = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        _global_state["best_score"] = float(s)


def rectify(a, L):
    a = a.copy()
    st = L // 2
    if st <= 0:
        return a
    seq = a[::L]
    for i in range(1, L):
        a[i::L] = seq
    return a


def design_bank(n, pp, rng, K=14):
    u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = []

    def add(name, arr):
        arr = np.clip(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = arr.sum()
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0:
            arr = arr * (1.0 / s)
        out.append((name, arr))

    add("const", np.ones(n))
    for i in range(4):
        eps = 0.06 * (2.0 ** i)
        add("lin%.1f" % eps, eps + (1.0 - eps) * u)
    for i in range(3):
        amp = 0.4 * (1.0 + 0.5 * i)
        add("cos%.2f" % amp, 1.0 + amp * (0.5 * math.cos(2.0 * math.pi * u) - 0.5))
    for i in range(3):
        amp = 0.35 * (1.0 + 0.6 * i)
        add("halfsin%.2f" % amp, 0.1 + amp * np.sin(math.pi * u))
    add("trapezoid", np.minimum(u, 1.0 - u) * 3.0 + 0.08)
    z = smooth1d(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n), -2.2, 2.2), max(3, n // 900) + 1)
    add("randwave", 1.0 + 0.6 * z / (1.0 + np.abs(z).max()))
    for i in range(2):
        zz = smooth1d(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n), -2.0, 2.0), max(3, n // 500) + 1)
        add("randwave2_%d" % i, 1.0 + (0.25 + 0.2 * i) * zz / (1.0 + np.abs(zz).max()))

    if pp is not None and pp.size > 1:
        ppn = pp if pp.size == n else np.interp(u, np.linspace(0, 1, pp.size), pp)
        pn = normalize_sum1(np.clip(ppn, 0.0, 1000.0))
        add("parent", pn)
        for sgm in (0.05, 0.15, 0.4):
            noise = smooth1d(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n), -2.0, 2.0), max(3, n // 400) + 1)
            blend = (1.0 - sgm) * pn + sgm * (1.0 + 0.8 * noise / (1.0 + np.abs(noise).max()))
            add("parent_n%.2f" % sgm, blend)

    while len(out) < K:
        zz = smooth1d(np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n), -2.0, 2.0), max(3, n // 700) + 1)
        add("rand_%d" % len(out), 1.0 + 0.5 * zz / (1.0 + np.abs(zz).max()))
    return out


def hill_descent(a, deadline, budget_frac, rng, K=10, iters=None):
    n = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).size
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0).copy()
    s0, t0, idx = score_info(a)
    best_a, best_s = a.copy(), s0
    save_best(best_a, best_s)
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = 0.02 / max(1, math.sqrt(n))
    step_L = max(1, int(round(n // 40.0)))
    stall = 0
    cnt = 0
    t_start = time.time()
    max_iters = iters if iters is not None else 800
    while time.time() < deadline:
        cnt += 1
        if cnt > max_iters:
            break
        elapsed = time.time() - t_start
        if elapsed > max(30.0, budget_frac * 60.0):
            break
        g = subgrad_step(a, idx, K * 2, n, step_L)
        ng = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if np.isfinite(ng) and ng > 0:
            mom = 0.5 * mom + 0.5 * (g / (ng + 1e-15))
        nbr = float(np.linalg.norm(mom))
        accepted = False
        if np.isfinite(nbr) and nbr > 0:
            d = mom / nbr
            d -= d.mean()
            cand = a + step * d
            cand[cand < 0] = 0
            s1, t1, idx1 = score_info(cand)
            if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_s - 1e-10:
                best_a, best_s = cand.copy(), s1
                save_best(best_a, best_s)
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
            else:
                cand2 = a + 0.5 * step * d
                cand2[cand2 < 0] = 0
                s2, t2, idx2 = score_info(cand2)
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_s - 1e-10:
                    best_a, best_s = cand2.copy(), s2
                    save_best(best_a, best_s)
                    stall = 0
                    accepted = True
            if not accepted:
                a = best_a
                idx = idx1 if np.isfinite(s1) else idx
            else:
                a = best_a
                idx = idx1
        else:
            a = best_a
            s0c, t0c, idx = score_info(a)
        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 40:
                break
        step *= 0.985
        if step < 1e-7:
            break
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    sf, tf, idxf = score_info(a)
    if np.isfinite(sf) and sf < best_s:
        best_a, best_s = a, sf
        save_best(best_a, best_s)
    return best_a, best_s


def analyze_best(pp):
    """Log the most relevant diagnostics for the initial (2.0) sequence."""
    try:
        n = np.asarray(pp, dtype=np.float64).size
        b = convolution_full(clamp_sequence(pp))
        s = float(clamp_sequence(pp).sum())
        print("Diag: n=%d bmax=%.6f at idx %s  (n-1=%d)" % (n, b.max(), np.argwhere(b == b.max())[:3].tolist(), n - 1))
        print("Diag: S^2=%.6g  S^2/n=%.6g  maxb=(%.6g)S^2/(n)" % (s * s, s * s / n, b.max() * n / (s * s)))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        print("Analysis unavailable: %s" % exc)


def main_search(total_deadline):
    pp = None
    try:
        pp = clamp_sequence(height_sequence_1)
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        pp = None
    if pp is None:
        pp = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64)

    s0, t0, idx0 = score_info(pp)
    print("Parent %s with shape_len %d" % ("loaded" if pp.size > 64 else "fallback", pp.size))
    print("Parent score=%.6f t=%.6f g0=%.6f g1=%.6f" %
          (s0, t0,
           float(np.dot(pp / pp.sum(), np.roll(pp, -1) / pp.sum())),
           float(np.dot(pp / pp.sum(), np.roll(pp, 1) / pp.sum()))))
    analyze_best(pp)
    save_best(pp, s0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240501)
    n = int(pp.size)
    # honor a near length-6962 parent but stay inside FFT power budget
    if n % 128 != 0:
        n = int(round(n / 128.0)) * 128
        n = max(256, min(n, 8192))
    budget = max(5.0, (total_deadline - time.time()) * 0.83)

    bank = design_bank(n, pp if pp.size == n else None, rng)
    bank.sort(key=lambda kv: score_info(normalize_sum1(kv[1]))[0])
    seed_pool = [normalize_sum1(b[1]) for b in bank[: min(9, len(bank))]]

    t安全防护 = time.time()
    while time.time() < total_deadline:
        tag = 0
        for base in seed_pool:
            if time.time() >= total_deadline - 2:
                break
            a = base.copy()
            if rng.random() < 0.25 and tag > 0:
                noise = smooth1d(rng.normal(0, 0.35, size=n), max(3, n // 350) + 1)
                a = 0.85 * a + 0.15 * (1.0 + noise)
                a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            a, sb = hill_descent(a, total_deadline, budget / (len(seed_pool) + 2.0), rng,
                                 K=int(rng.integers(6, 14)), iters=None)
            if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < 1.55:
                print("Found particularly good sequence score %.6f" % sb)
            tag += 1
    if _global_state["best"] is None:
        _global_state["best"] = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        _global_state["best_score"] = math.inf
    return _global_state["best"].copy()


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search with active-set descent; always return a valid list of floats."""
    budget_s = float(budget_s if budget_s is not None else 2400)
    deadline = time.time() + max(10.0, budget_s - 8.0)
    t_out = time.time()
    status = {"t": 0.0}

    def verbose(msg):
        now = time.time()
        if now - status["t"] > 1.0:
            status["t"] = now
            print("[%6.1fs] %s" % (now - t_out, msg))

    try:
        best = main_search(deadline)
        best = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
        best = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if best.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best)) or best.sum() < 0.02:
            elev = float(evaluate_sequence(best.tolist()))
            if not np.isfinite(elev):
                best = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        verbose("done; returning len=%d" % best.size)
        return [float(x) for x in best]
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        verbose("exception %s" % exc)
        try:
            fb = clamp_sequence(height_sequence_1)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            fb = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64)
        if fb is None:
            fb = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64)
        fb = np.clip(fb, 0.0, 1000.0)
        fb = fb * (1.0 / fb.sum())
        return [float(x) for x in fb]
```
